    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context
                    )

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )

                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )

                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:

                            cursor,

                            effective_parameters,
                            context,

                            break

        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context
                    )

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:

                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context
                    )

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context
                    )

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )

                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters

    def get_storage_encryption_key(key: bytes | str | None = None) -> bytes:
        """Resolve active 256-bit encryption key from parameter, settings, or environment."""
        if key is not None:
            return _parse_key_bytes(key)
    
        configured = settings.storage_encryption_key or os.getenv("STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY", "")
        if configured.strip():
            return _parse_key_bytes(configured)

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break

                        cursor, str_statement, context

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context
                    )

    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:

            if generic_setinputsizes:

                    )

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters

                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:

                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )

                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )

                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break

>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context
                    )

    def _handle_exception(self, error):
        if self._connection.is_closed():
            self._transaction = None
            self._started = False
    
        if not isinstance(error, AsyncAdapt_asyncpg_dbapi.Error):
            exception_mapping = self.dbapi._asyncpg_error_translate
    
            for super_ in type(error).__mro__:
                if super_ in exception_mapping:
                    translated_error = exception_mapping[super_](
                        "%s: %s" % (type(error), error)
                    )
                    translated_error.pgcode = translated_error.sqlstate = (
                        getattr(error, "sqlstate", None)
                    )

    @staticmethod
    def _tesseract_image_data(image_path: str) -> tuple[str, float | None, list[dict[str, object]]]:
        import math
    
        import numpy as np
        import pytesseract
        from PIL import Image
    
        from app.config import settings as _settings
        from app.providers.ocr.preprocessing import preprocess_image_with_transform
    
        pytesseract.pytesseract.tesseract_cmd = _tesseract_executable()
        with Image.open(image_path) as _raw_image:
            original_size = _raw_image.size
            # Item 3: normalise non-standard TIFF modes before any other step
            image = _raw_image
            if image.mode in ("I;16", "I;16B", "I", "F"):
                arr = np.array(image, dtype=np.float32)
                arr = (arr - arr.min()) / (arr.max() - arr.min() + 1e-9) * 255
                image = Image.fromarray(arr.astype(np.uint8), mode="L")
            elif image.mode == "CMYK":
                image = image.convert("RGB")
            elif image.mode == "P":
                image = image.convert("RGBA").convert("RGB")
    
            # Item 1: preprocessing pipeline (deskew / denoise / contrast / binarize)
            image, deskew_angle = preprocess_image_with_transform(image, _settings)
    
            # Item 2: memory cap � downscale oversized images
            w, h = image.size
            pre_resize_size = (w, h)
            pixels = w * h
            if pixels > _settings.ocr_max_image_pixels:
                scale = math.sqrt(_settings.ocr_max_image_pixels / pixels)
                new_w = max(1, int(w * scale))
                new_h = max(1, int(h * scale))
                image = image.resize((new_w, new_h), Image.LANCZOS)
    
            data = pytesseract.image_to_data(image, output_type=pytesseract.Output.DICT)
        words = []
        confidences = []
        word_boxes = []
        final_w, final_h = image.size
        for index, (word, raw_confidence) in enumerate(zip(data.get("text", []), data.get("conf", []))):
            if not word or not word.strip():
                continue
            words.append(word)
